575                     wire [$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] addr_s2addr_begin_diff;

577                     
578                     assign addr_s2addr_begin_diff = head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: $clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)] - rsp_addr_begin[$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0];
579                     
580                     // For the first unaligned INCR beat, the number of consumed request bytes is
581                     // NBYTEPERWORD - address_offset.  rsp_addr_end is an inclusive last-byte
582                     // address, so subtracting current_addr from it loses one byte and caused the
583                     // observed 0x...579 -> 0x...57e update instead of 0x...580.
584                     assign incr_advance_bytes =
585                         (addr_s2addr_begin_diff != 'd0) ?
586                             NBYTEPERWORD - {1'b0, addr_s2addr_begin_diff} :
587                             NBYTEPERWORD;
588                     
589                     integer c;
590                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
591        1/1              if (resetn == 1'b0) 
592        1/1                  for (c=0 ; c<HEAD_BUFF_DEEP ; c=c+1)
593        1/1                      head_buffer[c] <= #DLY 'd0;
594                         else begin
595        1/1                  if(del_head_en == 1'b1) begin         //删除操作
596        1/1                      head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][0] <= #DLY 1'b0; //将rsp_head_buff_index的used位清0
597        1/1                      head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_TIMOUT_OFFSET] <= #DLY 1'b0; 
598                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
599        1/1                  if(rspo2reqo_rhead_en == 1'b1) begin   //每个response packet的HEAD更新status
600        1/1                      head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_STAT_OFFSET +: 2] <= #DLY CONT;
601        1/1                      if(rspo2reqo_timout == 1'b1) begin
602        1/1                          head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_TIMOUT_OFFSET] <= #DLY 1'b1;
603                                 end
                        MISSING_ELSE
604                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
605                     
606                             // 地址必须按每个被接收的AXI读响应beat推进，而不能只在RKNP packet
607                             // 的HEAD推进。一个packet可能包含多个连续beat；只更新一次会使下一
608                             // 个交织packet的header地址落后一个或多个NBYTEPERWORD。
609        1/1                  if(rspo2reqo_rsp_valid == 1'b1 &&
610                                rspo2reqo_head_index[TAG_CNT_WITH +: 2] == 2'b00) begin
611        1/1                      if(head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_BURST_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b00) begin  //INCR类型
612        1/1                          head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]
613                                         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]
614                                                   + incr_advance_bytes;
615        1/1                          head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH]
616                                         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH]
617                                                   - incr_advance_bytes;
618                                 end
619                                 else begin   //WRAP类型
620                                     // rsp_addr_end is the address of the last valid byte, while
621                                     // head_buffer stores an AXI beat start address.  Wrap after
622                                     // the final beat start instead of comparing unlike addresses.
623        1/1                          if(head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]
624                                        == rsp_addr_end - (NBYTEPERWORD - 1))
625        1/1                              head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] <= #DLY rsp_addr_begin;
626                                     else
627        1/1                              head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] + NBYTEPERWORD;
628                                 end
629                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
630        1/1                  if(rknp_xx2reqo_head == 1'b1 && reqo2rknp_xx_ready == 1'b1)
631        1/1                      head_buffer[idle_head_buff_index] <= #DLY {
                        MISSING_ELSE
632                                     urg, 
633                                     iid, 
634                                     tid, 
635                                     ordkey,
636                                     addr, 
637                                     user, 
638                                     status,
639                                     wa2reqo_offset_addr,
640                                     len,
641                                     1'b0,
642                                     opc[1:0],
643                                     axid, 
644                                     opc[3:2],
645                                     tag_cnt2buffer,  
646                                     1'b1 //used
647                                 };
648                         end
649                     end
650                     
651                     
652                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
653                     //  检索buffer条目是否唯一操作
654                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
655                     wire [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] uniq_head_buff_index_hot;
656                     
657                     //生成检索码
658                     generate 
659                         for (i = 0 ; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP ; i = i + 1) begin 
660                             assign uniq_head_buff_index_hot[i] = 
661                                 ((rspo2reqo_head_index[TAG_CNT_WITH +: AXID_WITH+2] == head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: AXID_WITH+2]) 
662                                 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1) 
663                                 ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;
664                         end
665                     endgenerate

365        integer a;
                      
366        always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin

370            end else if(rknp_xx2reqo_head == 1'b1 && reqo2rknp_xx_ready == 1'b1) begin
                                                                                          
371                if (tag_name_index_hot == 'd0)              //没有找到对应的tag_name
                                                                                               
372                    if(tag_cnt[idle_tag_name_index] < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1'b1)

374                    else
                            
375                        tag_cnt[idle_tag_name_index] <= #DLY 'd0; 
                                                                      
376                else                                        //找到对应的tag_name
                                                                                         
377                    if(tag_cnt[tag_name_index] < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1'b1)

381            end
                   
382                
                    
383        end

427                     
428        1/1                  if (del_head_en && uniq_type_flag && !keep_tag_for_same_type)
429        1/1                      tag_name[del_tag_name_index][0] <= #DLY 1'b0;
                        MISSING_ELSE
430                         end
431                     end
432                     
433                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
434                     //  tag_name输出
435                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
436                     generate
437                         for(i=0; i<HEAD_BUFF_DEEP; i=i+1) begin
438                             assign reqo2rspo_tag_name[(i+1)*(AXID_WITH+3)-1:i*(AXID_WITH+3)] = tag_name[i];
439                         end
440                     endgenerate
441                     
442                     // 2:status, 8:offset_addr, 4:opc, 1:used, 1:timout
443                     localparam HEAD_BUFF_WITH = URGE_WITH + IID_WITH + TID_WITH + ADDR_WITH + AXID_WITH + LEN_WITH + USER_WITH + TAG_CNT_WITH + ORDKEY_WITH + 2 + 8 + 4 + 1 + 1;
444                     reg [HEAD_BUFF_WITH-1:0] head_buffer [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0];
445                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
446                     //  buffer空闲单元索引操作
447                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
448                     wire [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] head_buffer_used; //生成idle_head_buffer_index_hot的中间变量,被使用了则为0，没使用则为1,与buffer中的used位相反
449                     wire [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] idle_head_buff_index_hot;
450                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1:0] idle_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1 [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1 : 0]; 
451                     wire [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] idle_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2 [INDEX_WITH-1 : 0];
452                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1:0] idle_head_buff_index;
453                     //索引buffer为idle的单元
454                     generate 
455                         for (i = 0 ; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP ; i = i + 1) begin 
456                             assign head_buffer_used[i] = ~head_buffer[i][0];
457                         end
458                     endgenerate
459                     //保留二进制最低位1操作-生成独热码
460                     assign idle_head_buff_index_hot = head_buffer_used & (~head_buffer_used+1);
461                     //独热码转二进制码
462                     generate
463                     	for(i = 0; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
464                     		assign idle_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1[i] = idle_head_buff_index_hot[i]? i:'b0;
465                     	end
466                     endgenerate
467                     generate
468                     	for(i = 0; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
469                     		for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1) begin  
470                     			assign idle_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2[j][i] = idle_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1[i][j];
471                     		end
472                     	end
473                     endgenerate
474                     generate
475                     	for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1)begin 
476                     		assign idle_head_buff_index[j] = |idle_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2[j];
477                     	end
478                     endgenerate
479                     
480                     
481                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
482                     //  buffer条目索引操作
483                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
484                     wire [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] rsp_head_buff_index_hot;
485                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1:0] rsp_head_buff_index;
486                     //独热码转二进制码
487                     wire [INDEX_WITH-1 : 0] rsp_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1 [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1 : 0];
488                     wire [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1 : 0] rsp_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2 [INDEX_WITH-1 : 0];
489                     //生成独热码
490                     generate 
491                         for (i = 0 ; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP ; i = i + 1) begin 
492                             assign rsp_head_buff_index_hot[i] = ({rspo2reqo_head_index , 1'b1} == head_buffer[i][0 +: AXID_WITH+TAG_CNT_WITH+3]) ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;
493                         end
494                     endgenerate
495                     //独热码转二进制码
496                     generate
497                     	for(i = 0; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
498                     		assign rsp_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1[i] = rsp_head_buff_index_hot[i]? i:'b0;
499                     	end
500                     endgenerate
501                     generate
502                     	for(i = 0; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP; i = i+1)begin 
503                     		for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1) begin  
504                     			assign rsp_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2[j][i] = rsp_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp1[i][j];
505                     		end
506                     	end
507                     endgenerate
508                     generate
509                     	for(j = 0; j < INDEX_WITH; j = j+1)begin 
510                     		assign rsp_head_buff_index[j] = |rsp_head_buff_index_hot2bin_temp2[j];
511                     	end
512                     endgenerate
513                     
514                     
515                     
516                     
517                     
518                     
519                     
520                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
521                     //  1、buffer写入操作
522                     //  2、buffer更新status操作 
523                     //  3、buffer更新addr操作
524                     //  4、buffer删除操作
525                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
526                     
527                     //head line buffer的各个域段的偏移量参数
528                     localparam BUFF_TAGCNT_OFFSET = 1;
529                     localparam BUFF_OPC_OFFSET = BUFF_TAGCNT_OFFSET + TAG_CNT_WITH;
530                     localparam BUFF_AXID_OFFSET = BUFF_OPC_OFFSET + 2;  //opc[3:2]
531                     localparam BUFF_BURST_OFFSET = BUFF_AXID_OFFSET + AXID_WITH;  
532                     localparam BUFF_TIMOUT_OFFSET = BUFF_BURST_OFFSET + 2; //opc[1:0]
533                     localparam BUFF_LEN_OFFSET = BUFF_TIMOUT_OFFSET + 1;
534                     localparam BUFF_OFFADDR_OFFSET = BUFF_LEN_OFFSET + LEN_WITH;
535                     localparam BUFF_STAT_OFFSET = BUFF_OFFADDR_OFFSET + 8;
536                     localparam BUFF_USER_OFFSET = BUFF_STAT_OFFSET + 2;
537                     localparam BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET = BUFF_USER_OFFSET + USER_WITH;
538                     localparam BUFF_ORDKEY_OFFSET = BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET + ADDR_WITH;
539                     localparam BUFF_TID_OFFSET = BUFF_ORDKEY_OFFSET + ORDKEY_WITH;
540                     localparam BUFF_IID_OFFSET = BUFF_TID_OFFSET + TID_WITH;
541                     localparam BUFF_URGE_OFFSET = BUFF_IID_OFFSET + IID_WITH;
542                     
543                     
544                     
545                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
546                     //  计算响应地址边界
547                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
548                     wire [ADDR_WITH-1:0] rsp_addr_begin;
549                     wire [ADDR_WITH-1:0] rsp_addr_end;
550                     
551                     addr_border_cout#(
552                          .ADDR_WITH      (ADDR_WITH     )
553                         ,.LEN_WITH       (LEN_WITH      )
554                         ,.NBYTEPERWORD   (NBYTEPERWORD  )
555                         ,.ADDR_BLOCK_SIZE(1             ) //1byte为一个地址块
556                         ,.COUNT_MODE     (1             ) //表示计算的是对齐地址边界
557                     )U_ADDR_BORDER_COUNT_RSP(
558                          .burst          (head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_BURST_OFFSET +: 2]          )   
559                         ,.addr           (head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]   )   
560                         ,.len            (head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH]     )  
561                         ,.addr_begin     (rsp_addr_begin                                                    )  
562                         ,.addr_end       (rsp_addr_end                                                      ) 
563                     );
564                     
565                     
566                     
567                     reg [INDEX_WITH-1:0] idle_head_buff_index_d; //idle_head_buff_index_d打一拍
568                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
569        1/1              if (resetn == 1'b0) 
570        1/1                  idle_head_buff_index_d <= #DLY 1'b0;
571                         else
572        1/1                  idle_head_buff_index_d <= #DLY idle_head_buff_index;
573                     end
574                     
575                     wire [$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0] addr_s2addr_begin_diff;
576                     wire [$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD):0]   incr_advance_bytes;
577                     
578                     assign addr_s2addr_begin_diff = head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: $clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)] - rsp_addr_begin[$clog2(NBYTEPERWORD)-1:0];
579                     
580                     // For the first unaligned INCR beat, the number of consumed request bytes is
581                     // NBYTEPERWORD - address_offset.  rsp_addr_end is an inclusive last-byte
582                     // address, so subtracting current_addr from it loses one byte and caused the
583                     // observed 0x...579 -> 0x...57e update instead of 0x...580.
584                     assign incr_advance_bytes =
585                         (addr_s2addr_begin_diff != 'd0) ?
586                             NBYTEPERWORD - {1'b0, addr_s2addr_begin_diff} :
587                             NBYTEPERWORD;
588                     
589                     integer c;
590                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
591        1/1              if (resetn == 1'b0) 
592        1/1                  for (c=0 ; c<HEAD_BUFF_DEEP ; c=c+1)
593        1/1                      head_buffer[c] <= #DLY 'd0;
594                         else begin
595        1/1                  if(del_head_en == 1'b1) begin         //删除操作
596        1/1                      head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][0] <= #DLY 1'b0; //将rsp_head_buff_index的used位清0
597        1/1                      head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_TIMOUT_OFFSET] <= #DLY 1'b0; 
598                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
599        1/1                  if(rspo2reqo_rhead_en == 1'b1) begin   //每个response packet的HEAD更新status
600        1/1                      head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_STAT_OFFSET +: 2] <= #DLY CONT;
601        1/1                      if(rspo2reqo_timout == 1'b1) begin
602        1/1                          head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_TIMOUT_OFFSET] <= #DLY 1'b1;
603                                 end
                        MISSING_ELSE
604                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
605                     
606                             // 地址必须按每个被接收的AXI读响应beat推进，而不能只在RKNP packet
607                             // 的HEAD推进。一个packet可能包含多个连续beat；只更新一次会使下一
608                             // 个交织packet的header地址落后一个或多个NBYTEPERWORD。
609        1/1                  if(rspo2reqo_rsp_valid == 1'b1 &&
610                                rspo2reqo_head_index[TAG_CNT_WITH +: 2] == 2'b00) begin
611        1/1                      if(head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_BURST_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b00) begin  //INCR类型
612        1/1                          head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]
613                                         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]
614                                                   + incr_advance_bytes;
615        1/1                          head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH]
616                                         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH]
617                                                   - incr_advance_bytes;
618                                 end
619                                 else begin   //WRAP类型
620                                     // rsp_addr_end is the address of the last valid byte, while
621                                     // head_buffer stores an AXI beat start address.  Wrap after
622                                     // the final beat start instead of comparing unlike addresses.
623        1/1                          if(head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]
624                                        == rsp_addr_end - (NBYTEPERWORD - 1))
625        1/1                              head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] <= #DLY rsp_addr_begin;
626                                     else
627        1/1                              head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] + NBYTEPERWORD;
628                                 end
629                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
630        1/1                  if(rknp_xx2reqo_head == 1'b1 && reqo2rknp_xx_ready == 1'b1)
631        1/1                      head_buffer[idle_head_buff_index] <= #DLY {
                        MISSING_ELSE
632                                     urg, 
633                                     iid, 
634                                     tid, 
635                                     ordkey,
636                                     addr, 
637                                     user, 
638                                     status,
639                                     wa2reqo_offset_addr,
640                                     len,
641                                     1'b0,
642                                     opc[1:0],
643                                     axid, 
644                                     opc[3:2],
645                                     tag_cnt2buffer,  
646                                     1'b1 //used
647                                 };
648                         end
649                     end
650                     
651                     
652                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
653                     //  检索buffer条目是否唯一操作
654                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
655                     wire [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] uniq_head_buff_index_hot;
656                     
657                     //生成检索码
658                     generate 
659                         for (i = 0 ; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP ; i = i + 1) begin 
660                             assign uniq_head_buff_index_hot[i] = 
661                                 ((rspo2reqo_head_index[TAG_CNT_WITH +: AXID_WITH+2] == head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: AXID_WITH+2]) 
662                                 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1) 
663                                 ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;
664                         end
665                     endgenerate
666                     
667                     assign uniq_type_flag = (uniq_head_buff_index_hot == rsp_head_buff_index_hot) ? 1'b1 : 1'b0;
668                     
669                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
670                     //  buffer读取操作
671                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
672                     
673                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin    
674        1/1              if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
675        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_urg <= #DLY 'd0;
676        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_addr <= #DLY 'd0;
677        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_ordkey <= #DLY 'd0;
678        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_len <= #DLY 'd0;
679        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_user <= #DLY 'd0;
680        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_iid <= #DLY 'd0;
681        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_tid <= #DLY 'd0;
682        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_status <= #DLY 'd0;
683        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_offset_addr <= #DLY 8'd0;
684        1/1                  reqo2rspo_timout <= #DLY 1'b0;
685        1/1              end else if(rspo2reqo_rhead_en == 1'b1) begin
686        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_urg         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_URGE_OFFSET +: URGE_WITH];
687        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_addr        <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH];
688        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_ordkey      <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ORDKEY_OFFSET +: ORDKEY_WITH];
689        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_len         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH];
690        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_user        <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_USER_OFFSET +: USER_WITH];
691        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_iid         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_IID_OFFSET +: IID_WITH];
692        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_tid         <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_TID_OFFSET +: TID_WITH];
693        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_status      <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_STAT_OFFSET +: 2];
694        1/1                  reqo2rspo_rsp_offset_addr <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_OFFADDR_OFFSET +: 8];
695        1/1                  reqo2rspo_timout          <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_TIMOUT_OFFSET];
696                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
697                     end
698                     
699                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
700                     //  生成fir_req_flag信号-检测当前请求是否为该类型的第一笔请求，用于后续ERR REQ保序
701                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
702                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
703        1/1              if (resetn == 1'b0) 
704        1/1                  fir_req_flag <= #DLY 1'b0;
705        1/1              else if(rknp_xx2reqo_head == 1'b1 && reqo2rknp_xx_ready == 1'b1) begin
706        1/1                  if(tag_name_index_hot == 'd0) //没有找到对应的tag_name,说明当前buffer中无该类型的请求
707        1/1                      fir_req_flag <= #DLY 1'b1;
708        1/1                  else if(del_head_en == 1'b1 && uniq_type_flag == 1'b1 && tag_name_index == del_tag_name_index) 
709        1/1                      fir_req_flag <= #DLY 1'b1;  //删除bvuffer中唯一一笔该类型请求的同时接收一笔该类型请求，也视为第一笔请求
710                             else
711        1/1                      fir_req_flag <= #DLY 1'b0;
712        1/1              end else if(rspo2reqo_ready == 1'b1) begin
713        1/1                  fir_req_flag <= #DLY 1'b0; 
714                         end
                        MISSING_ELSE
715                     end
716                     
717                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
718                     //  生成reqo2rsp_tag_cnt信号-将tag_cnt传递给下游，用于后续ERR REQ保序
719                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
720                     always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
721        1/1              if (resetn == 1'b0) 
722        1/1                  reqo2rspo_tag_cnt <= #DLY 1'b0;
723        1/1              else if(rknp_xx2reqo_head == 1'b1 && reqo2rknp_xx_ready == 1'b1)
724        1/1                  reqo2rspo_tag_cnt <= #DLY tag_cnt2buffer;
                        MISSING_ELSE
725                     end
726                     
727                     
728                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
729                     //  计算请求地址边界
730                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
731                     wire [ADDR_WITH-$clog2(ADDR_BLOCK_SIZE)-1:0] req_addr_begin;
732                     wire [ADDR_WITH-$clog2(ADDR_BLOCK_SIZE)-1:0] req_addr_end;
733                     generate 
734                         if(ADDR_BP_TYPE == 0) begin
735                            
736                         end else begin
737                             addr_border_cout#(
738                                  .ADDR_WITH      (ADDR_WITH     )
739                                 ,.LEN_WITH       (LEN_WITH      )
740                                 ,.NBYTEPERWORD   (NBYTEPERWORD  )
741                                 ,.ADDR_BLOCK_SIZE(ADDR_BLOCK_SIZE   )
742                                 ,.COUNT_MODE     (0                 ) //表示计算的是原始地址边界
743                             )U_ADDR_BORDER_COUNT_REQ(
744                                  .burst          (opc[1:0]          )   
745                                 ,.addr           (addr              )   
746                                 ,.len            (len               )  
747                                 ,.addr_begin     (req_addr_begin    )  
748                                 ,.addr_end       (req_addr_end      ) 
749                             );
750                         end
751                     
752                     endgenerate
753                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
754                     //  请求地址边界存储功能
755                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
756                     reg [ADDR_WITH-$clog2(ADDR_BLOCK_SIZE)-1:0] addr_begin_buffer [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0];
757                     reg [ADDR_WITH-$clog2(ADDR_BLOCK_SIZE)-1:0] addr_end_buffer [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0];
758                     reg [SUBR_WITH-1:0] subr_buffer [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0];
759                     generate 
760                         if(ADDR_BP_TYPE == 0)begin  //无同地址反压
761                     
762                         end else begin              //开启同地址反压
763                             integer d;
764                             always @(posedge clk or negedge resetn) begin
765        1/1                      if(resetn == 1'b0) begin
766        1/1                          for(d=0 ; d<HEAD_BUFF_DEEP ; d=d+1) begin
767        1/1                              addr_begin_buffer[d] <= #DLY 'd0;
768        1/1                              addr_end_buffer[d] <= #DLY 'd0;
769                                     end
770                                 end else begin
771        1/1                          if(rknp_xx2reqo_head == 1'b1 && reqo2rknp_xx_ready == 1'b1) begin  //写入操作
772        1/1                              addr_begin_buffer[idle_head_buff_index] <= #DLY req_addr_begin;
773        1/1                              addr_end_buffer[idle_head_buff_index] <= #DLY req_addr_end;
774        1/1                              subr_buffer[idle_head_buff_index] <= #DLY subr;
775                                     end
                        MISSING_ELSE
776                                 end
777                             end
778                         end
779                     endgenerate
780                     
781                     
782                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
783                     //  同地址反压功能
784                     //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
785                     
786                     wire overlop_flag;  //存在地址重合标志位
787                     reg [HEAD_BUFF_DEEP-1:0] overlop_temp;//对比单个单元地址重合
788                     generate 
789                         if(ADDR_BP_TYPE == 1)begin  //写后写，写后读同地址反压
790                             for(i=0 ; i < HEAD_BUFF_DEEP; i=i+1) begin
791                                 always @(*) begin
792        1/1                          if(head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b01 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1)                              //写后读，写后写保序
793        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = (req_addr_end < addr_begin_buffer[i] || req_addr_begin > addr_end_buffer[i] || subr_buffer[i] != subr) ? 1'b0 : 1'b1;
794                                         
795                                     else 
796        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = 1'b0;
***repeat 1
792        1/1                          if(head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b01 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1)                              //写后读，写后写保序
793        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = (req_addr_end < addr_begin_buffer[i] || req_addr_begin > addr_end_buffer[i] || subr_buffer[i] != subr) ? 1'b0 : 1'b1;
794                                         
795                                     else 
796        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = 1'b0;
***repeat 2
792        1/1                          if(head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b01 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1)                              //写后读，写后写保序
793        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = (req_addr_end < addr_begin_buffer[i] || req_addr_begin > addr_end_buffer[i] || subr_buffer[i] != subr) ? 1'b0 : 1'b1;
794                                         
795                                     else 
796        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = 1'b0;
***repeat 3
792        1/1                          if(head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b01 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1)                              //写后读，写后写保序
793        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = (req_addr_end < addr_begin_buffer[i] || req_addr_begin > addr_end_buffer[i] || subr_buffer[i] != subr) ? 1'b0 : 1'b1;
794                                         
795                                     else 
796        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = 1'b0;
***repeat 4
792        1/1                          if(head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b01 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1)                              //写后读，写后写保序
793        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = (req_addr_end < addr_begin_buffer[i] || req_addr_begin > addr_end_buffer[i] || subr_buffer[i] != subr) ? 1'b0 : 1'b1;
794                                         
795                                     else 
796        1/1                              overlop_temp[i] = 1'b0;
***repeat 5
792        1/1                          if(head_buffer[i][BUFF_OPC_OFFSET +: 2] == 2'b01 && head_buffer[i][0] == 1'b1)                              //写后读，写后写保序

552             .ADDR_WITH      (ADDR_WITH     )

                           ==>

606                // 地址必须按每个被接收的AXI读响应beat推进，而不能只在RKNP packet
607                // 的HEAD推进。一个packet可能包含多个连续beat；只更新一次会使下一
608                // 个交织packet的header地址落后一个或多个NBYTEPERWORD。
609                if(rspo2reqo_rsp_valid == 1'b1 &&
                   -5-

                           ==>

614                                      + incr_advance_bytes;
615                        head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH]
616                            <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_LEN_OFFSET +: LEN_WITH]
617                                      - incr_advance_bytes;
618                    end
619                    else begin   //WRAP类型
620                        // rsp_addr_end is the address of the last valid byte, while
621                        // head_buffer stores an AXI beat start address.  Wrap after
622                        // the final beat start instead of comparing unlike addresses.
623                        if(head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH]
                           -7-  
624                           == rsp_addr_end - (NBYTEPERWORD - 1))
625                            head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] <= #DLY rsp_addr_begin;
                               ==>
626                        else
627                            head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] <= #DLY head_buffer[rsp_head_buff_index][BUFF_ADDR_OFFSET +: ADDR_WITH] + NBYTEPERWORD;
                               ==>
628                    end
629                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
630                if(rknp_xx2reqo_head == 1'b1 && reqo2rknp_xx_ready == 1'b1)
